What brings you in?\n\nPatient: Lately, I've been feeling extremely overwhelmed by these persistent, intrusive worries that I just can't seem to shake. I get this intense fear that something terrible will happen, like a break-in, a fire, or that I'll get dangerously contaminated. The only way to make that unbearable tension go away is to just give in and do the ritual.\n\nDoctor: Did these symptoms start after using any drugs, starting a new medication, or having a medical or neurological problem?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't started any new medications or used any drugs. I also don't have any medical or neurological issues that I know of before these symptoms began.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had tics, like repeated blinking, facial movements, throat clearing, or other sudden movements or sounds you couldn’t control?\n\nPatient: No, I have never experienced tics, sudden uncontrolled movements, or repetitive sounds. In the moment, they just feel like things I absolutely have to do.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been having these intrusive thoughts and repeating these behaviors?\n\nPatient: I'm not entirely sure how long it has been going on, as I haven't kept track of the exact timeline. It feels like it has been happening for quite a while, but I can't pinpoint when it first started.\n\nDoctor: If you try not to do the checking or cleaning, what happens—do you become very anxious or feel like something bad will happen?\n\nPatient: Yes, if I try to stop myself, the anxiety and dread become absolutely overwhelming. I get this intense fear that something terrible will happen, like a break-in, a fire, or that I'll get dangerously contaminated. The only way to make that unbearable tension go away is to just give in and do the ritual.\n\nDoctor: Did these symptoms start after using any drugs, starting a new medication, or having a medical or neurological problem?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't started any new medications or used any drugs. I also don't have any medical or neurological issues that I know of before these symptoms began.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had tics, like repeated blinking, facial movements, throat clearing, or other sudden movements or sounds you couldn’t control?\n\nPatient: No, I have never experienced tics, sudden uncontrolled movements, or repetitive sounds. Those aren't things I've ever had to deal with.\n\nDoctor: Have these fears or rituals made you avoid places, people, or activities, or caused problems getting to work, school, or leaving the house on time?\n\nPatient: To be honest, I haven't really thought about it that way. I'm not entirely sure if it has caused major avoidance or any significant delays in my daily routine.\n\nPatient: To be honest, I haven't really thought about it that way.
